     1→"""TinyTorch losses module.
     2→
     3→Extracted from CS249R Module 04: Loss Functions.
     4→NumPy-only implementation for educational purposes.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import numpy as np
    10→
    11→from cohezion.tinytorch.tensor import Tensor
    12→
    13→
    14→def mse_loss(predictions: Tensor, targets: Tensor) -> Tensor:
    15→    """Mean Squared Error loss for regression."""
    16→    diff = predictions.data - targets.data
    17→    return Tensor(np.mean(diff ** 2))
    18→
    19→
    20→def mse_loss_gradient(predictions: Tensor, targets: Tensor) -> Tensor:
    21→    """Gradient of MSE loss w.r.t. predictions."""
    22→    n = predictions.data.size
    23→    return Tensor(2.0 * (predictions.data - targets.data) / n)
    24→
    25→
    26→def cross_entropy_loss(logits: Tensor, targets: Tensor) -> Tensor:
    27→    """Cross-entropy loss for multi-class classification."""
    28→    shifted = logits.data - logits.data.max(axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    29→    exp_logits = np.exp(shifted)
    30→    probs = exp_logits / exp_logits.sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    31→    probs = np.clip(probs, 1e-7, 1 - 1e-7)
    32→
    33→    if targets.data.ndim == 1:
    34→        batch_size = logits.data.shape[0]
    35→        log_probs = -np.log(probs[np.arange(batch_size), targets.data.astype(int)])
    36→        return Tensor(np.mean(log_probs))
    37→    return Tensor(-np.mean(np.sum(targets.data * np.log(probs), axis=-1)))
    38→
    39→
    40→def binary_cross_entropy_loss(predictions: Tensor, targets: Tensor) -> Tensor:
    41→    """Binary cross-entropy for binary classification."""
    42→    p = np.clip(predictions.data, 1e-7, 1 - 1e-7)
    43→    loss = -(targets.data * np.log(p) + (1 - targets.data) * np.log(1 - p))
    44→    return Tensor(np.mean(loss))
    45→
    46→
    47→def huber_loss(predictions: Tensor, targets: Tensor, delta: float = 1.0) -> Tensor:
    48→    """Huber loss: MSE for small errors, MAE for large. Robust to outliers."""
    49→    diff = np.abs(predictions.data - targets.data)
    50→    loss = np.where(diff <= delta, 0.5 * diff ** 2, delta * (diff - 0.5 * delta))
    51→    return Tensor(np.mean(loss))
    52→
    53→
    54→def l1_loss(predictions: Tensor, targets: Tensor) -> Tensor:
    55→    """L1 (Mean Absolute Error) loss."""
    56→    return Tensor(np.mean(np.abs(predictions.data - targets.data)))
    57→
    58→
    59→LOSSES = {
    60→    "mse": mse_loss, "cross_entropy": cross_entropy_loss,
    61→    "binary_cross_entropy": binary_cross_entropy_loss,
    62→    "huber": huber_loss, "l1": l1_loss,
    63→}
    64→

     1→"""TinyTorch dataloader module.
     2→
     3→Extracted from CS249R Module 05: Data Loading.
     4→NumPy-only implementation for educational purposes.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import numpy as np
    10→
    11→from cohezion.tinytorch.tensor import Tensor
    12→
    13→
    14→class Dataset:
    15→    """Base dataset class. Subclass and implement __getitem__ and __len__."""
    16→
    17→    def __getitem__(self, idx: int) -> tuple:
    18→        raise NotImplementedError
    19→
    20→    def __len__(self) -> int:
    21→        raise NotImplementedError
    22→
    23→
    24→class TensorDataset(Dataset):
    25→    """Dataset wrapping tensors. Each sample is a tuple of corresponding elements."""
    26→
    27→    def __init__(self, *tensors: Tensor):
    28→        assert all(t.shape[0] == tensors[0].shape[0] for t in tensors)
    29→        self.tensors = tensors
    30→
    31→    def __getitem__(self, idx: int) -> tuple[Tensor, ...]:
    32→        return tuple(Tensor(t.data[idx]) for t in self.tensors)
    33→
    34→    def __len__(self) -> int:
    35→        return self.tensors[0].shape[0]
    36→
    37→
    38→class DataLoader:
    39→    """Iterable data loader with batching and shuffling."""
    40→
    41→    def __init__(self, dataset: Dataset, batch_size: int = 32, shuffle: bool = True):
    42→        self.dataset = dataset
    43→        self.batch_size = batch_size
    44→        self.shuffle = shuffle
    45→
    46→    def __iter__(self):
    47→        n = len(self.dataset)
    48→        indices = np.arange(n)
    49→        if self.shuffle:
    50→            np.random.shuffle(indices)
    51→
    52→        for start in range(0, n, self.batch_size):
    53→            batch_indices = indices[start:start + self.batch_size]
    54→            batch = [self.dataset[i] for i in batch_indices]
    55→
    56→            # Collate: stack each element across the batch
    57→            collated = []
    58→            for elem_idx in range(len(batch[0])):
    59→                stacked = np.stack([b[elem_idx].data for b in batch])
    60→                collated.append(Tensor(stacked))
    61→
    62→            yield tuple(collated)
    63→
    64→    def __len__(self) -> int:
    65→        return (len(self.dataset) + self.batch_size - 1) // self.batch_size
    66→
    67→
    68→def train_test_split(
    69→    *arrays: Tensor,
    70→    test_size: float = 0.2,
    71→    seed: int | None = None,
    72→) -> list[Tensor]:
    73→    """Split arrays into train and test sets."""
    74→    if seed is not None:
    75→        np.random.seed(seed)
    76→
    77→    n = arrays[0].shape[0]
    78→    indices = np.random.permutation(n)
    79→    split_idx = int(n * (1 - test_size))
    80→    train_idx, test_idx = indices[:split_idx], indices[split_idx:]
    81→
    82→    result = []
    83→    for arr in arrays:
    84→        result.append(Tensor(arr.data[train_idx]))
    85→        result.append(Tensor(arr.data[test_idx]))
    86→    return result
    87→

     1→"""TinyTorch autograd module.
     2→
     3→Extracted from CS249R Module 06: Automatic Differentiation.
     4→NumPy-only implementation for educational purposes.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import numpy as np
    10→
    11→
    12→class Value:
    13→    """Scalar value with automatic differentiation.
    14→
    15→    Tracks computation graph and computes gradients via backpropagation.
    16→    Inspired by Andrej Karpathy's micrograd.
    17→    """
    18→
    19→    def __init__(self, data: float, children: tuple = (), op: str = ""):
    20→        self.data = float(data)
    21→        self.grad = 0.0
    22→        self._backward = lambda: None
    23→        self._prev = set(children)
    24→        self._op = op
    25→
    26→    def __repr__(self):
    27→        return f"Value(data={self.data:.4f}, grad={self.grad:.4f})"
    28→
    29→    def __add__(self, other):
    30→        other = other if isinstance(other, Value) else Value(other)
    31→        out = Value(self.data + other.data, (self, other), "+")
    32→
    33→        def _backward():
    34→            self.grad += out.grad
    35→            other.grad += out.grad
    36→        out._backward = _backward
    37→        return out
    38→
    39→    def __radd__(self, other):
    40→        return self + other
    41→
    42→    def __mul__(self, other):
    43→        other = other if isinstance(other, Value) else Value(other)
    44→        out = Value(self.data * other.data, (self, other), "*")
    45→
    46→        def _backward():
    47→            self.grad += other.data * out.grad
    48→            other.grad += self.data * out.grad
    49→        out._backward = _backward
    50→        return out
    51→
    52→    def __rmul__(self, other):
    53→        return self * other
    54→
    55→    def __sub__(self, other):
    56→        return self + (-other)
    57→
    58→    def __rsub__(self, other):
    59→        return (-self) + other
    60→
    61→    def __neg__(self):
    62→        return self * -1
    63→
    64→    def __truediv__(self, other):
    65→        return self * other ** -1
    66→
    67→    def __pow__(self, exponent):
    68→        assert isinstance(exponent, (int, float))
    69→        out = Value(self.data ** exponent, (self,), f"**{exponent}")
    70→
    71→        def _backward():
    72→            self.grad += exponent * (self.data ** (exponent - 1)) * out.grad
    73→        out._backward = _backward
    74→        return out
    75→
    76→    def relu(self):
    77→        out = Value(max(0, self.data), (self,), "relu")
    78→
    79→        def _backward():
    80→            self.grad += (out.data > 0) * out.grad
    81→        out._backward = _backward
    82→        return out
    83→
    84→    def tanh(self):
    85→        t = np.tanh(self.data)
    86→        out = Value(t, (self,), "tanh")
    87→
    88→        def _backward():
    89→            self.grad += (1 - t ** 2) * out.grad
    90→        out._backward = _backward
    91→        return out
    92→
    93→    def exp(self):
    94→        e = np.exp(self.data)
    95→        out = Value(e, (self,), "exp")
    96→
    97→        def _backward():
    98→            self.grad += e * out.grad
    99→        out._backward = _backward
   100→        return out
   101→
   102→    def log(self):
   103→        out = Value(np.log(self.data), (self,), "log")
   104→
   105→        def _backward():
   106→            self.grad += (1.0 / self.data) * out.grad
   107→        out._backward = _backward
   108→        return out
   109→
   110→    def backward(self):
   111→        """Compute gradients via reverse-mode autodiff (backpropagation)."""
   112→        topo = []
   113→        visited = set()
   114→
   115→        def build_topo(v):
   116→            if v not in visited:
   117→                visited.add(v)
   118→                for child in v._prev:
   119→                    build_topo(child)
   120→                topo.append(v)
   121→
   122→        build_topo(self)
   123→        self.grad = 1.0
   124→
   125→        for v in reversed(topo):
   126→            v._backward()
   127→
   128→    def zero_grad(self):
   129→        """Reset gradient to zero."""
   130→        self.grad = 0.0
   131→
   132→
   133→class Neuron:
   134→    """Single neuron with weights, bias, and optional activation."""
   135→
   136→    def __init__(self, n_inputs: int, nonlin: bool = True):
   137→        self.w = [Value(np.random.uniform(-1, 1)) for _ in range(n_inputs)]
   138→        self.b = Value(0.0)
   139→        self.nonlin = nonlin
   140→
   141→    def __call__(self, x: list[Value]) -> Value:
   142→        act = sum((wi * xi for wi, xi in zip(self.w, x)), self.b)
   143→        return act.relu() if self.nonlin else act
   144→
   145→    def parameters(self) -> list[Value]:
   146→        return self.w + [self.b]
   147→
   148→
   149→class Layer:
   150→    """Layer of neurons."""
   151→
   152→    def __init__(self, n_inputs: int, n_outputs: int, nonlin: bool = True):
   153→        self.neurons = [Neuron(n_inputs, nonlin) for _ in range(n_outputs)]
   154→
   155→    def __call__(self, x: list[Value]) -> list[Value]:
   156→        out = [n(x) for n in self.neurons]
   157→        return out[0] if len(out) == 1 else out
   158→
   159→    def parameters(self) -> list[Value]:
   160→        return [p for n in self.neurons for p in n.parameters()]
   161→
   162→
   163→class MLP:
   164→    """Multi-layer perceptron using autograd Values."""
   165→
   166→    def __init__(self, n_inputs: int, layer_sizes: list[int]):
   167→        sizes = [n_inputs] + layer_sizes
   168→        self.layers = [
   169→            Layer(sizes[i], sizes[i + 1], nonlin=(i != len(layer_sizes) - 1))
   170→            for i in range(len(layer_sizes))
   171→        ]
   172→
   173→    def __call__(self, x: list[Value]) -> list[Value] | Value:
   174→        for layer in self.layers:
   175→            x = layer(x)
   176→        return x
   177→
   178→    def parameters(self) -> list[Value]:
   179→        return [p for layer in self.layers for p in layer.parameters()]
   180→
   181→    def zero_grad(self):
   182→        for p in self.parameters():
   183→            p.zero_grad()
   184→

     1→"""TinyTorch optimizers module.
     2→
     3→Extracted from CS249R Module 07: Optimizers.
     4→NumPy-only implementation for educational purposes.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import numpy as np
    10→
    11→from cohezion.tinytorch.tensor import Tensor
    12→
    13→
    14→class SGD:
    15→    """Stochastic Gradient Descent optimizer.
    16→
    17→    Updates parameters: w = w - lr * gradient
    18→    With momentum: v = momentum * v - lr * gradient; w = w + v
    19→    """
    20→
    21→    def __init__(self, parameters: list[Tensor], lr: float = 0.01, momentum: float = 0.0):
    22→        self.parameters = parameters
    23→        self.lr = lr
    24→        self.momentum = momentum
    25→        self.velocities = [np.zeros_like(p.data) for p in parameters]
    26→
    27→    def step(self, gradients: list[Tensor]):
    28→        for i, (param, grad) in enumerate(zip(self.parameters, gradients)):
    29→            if self.momentum > 0:
    30→                self.velocities[i] = self.momentum * self.velocities[i] - self.lr * grad.data
    31→                param.data += self.velocities[i]
    32→            else:
    33→                param.data -= self.lr * grad.data
    34→
    35→    def zero_grad(self):
    36→        pass  # Gradients managed externally in this simple implementation
    37→
    38→
    39→class Adam:
    40→    """Adam optimizer: adaptive learning rates with momentum.
    41→
    42→    Combines benefits of AdaGrad (adaptive) and RMSProp (momentum).
    43→    Default hyperparameters from the original paper (Kingma & Ba, 2015).
    44→    """
    45→
    46→    def __init__(
    47→        self,
    48→        parameters: list[Tensor],
    49→        lr: float = 0.001,
    50→        beta1: float = 0.9,
    51→        beta2: float = 0.999,
    52→        eps: float = 1e-8,
    53→    ):
    54→        self.parameters = parameters
    55→        self.lr = lr
    56→        self.beta1 = beta1
    57→        self.beta2 = beta2
    58→        self.eps = eps
    59→        self.t = 0
    60→        self.m = [np.zeros_like(p.data) for p in parameters]  # First moment
    61→        self.v = [np.zeros_like(p.data) for p in parameters]  # Second moment
    62→
    63→    def step(self, gradients: list[Tensor]):
    64→        self.t += 1
    65→        for i, (param, grad) in enumerate(zip(self.parameters, gradients)):
    66→            self.m[i] = self.beta1 * self.m[i] + (1 - self.beta1) * grad.data
    67→            self.v[i] = self.beta2 * self.v[i] + (1 - self.beta2) * grad.data ** 2
    68→
    69→            # Bias correction
    70→            m_hat = self.m[i] / (1 - self.beta1 ** self.t)
    71→            v_hat = self.v[i] / (1 - self.beta2 ** self.t)
    72→
    73→            param.data -= self.lr * m_hat / (np.sqrt(v_hat) + self.eps)
    74→
    75→
    76→class RMSProp:
    77→    """RMSProp: adaptive learning rate using running average of squared gradients."""
    78→
    79→    def __init__(
    80→        self,
    81→        parameters: list[Tensor],
    82→        lr: float = 0.001,
    83→        alpha: float = 0.99,
    84→        eps: float = 1e-8,
    85→    ):
    86→        self.parameters = parameters
    87→        self.lr = lr
    88→        self.alpha = alpha
    89→        self.eps = eps
    90→        self.v = [np.zeros_like(p.data) for p in parameters]
    91→
    92→    def step(self, gradients: list[Tensor]):
    93→        for i, (param, grad) in enumerate(zip(self.parameters, gradients)):
    94→            self.v[i] = self.alpha * self.v[i] + (1 - self.alpha) * grad.data ** 2
    95→            param.data -= self.lr * grad.data / (np.sqrt(self.v[i]) + self.eps)
    96→
    97→
    98→class LearningRateScheduler:
    99→    """Adjusts learning rate during training."""
   100→
   101→    @staticmethod
   102→    def step_decay(initial_lr: float, epoch: int, drop_rate: float = 0.5, epochs_drop: int = 10):
   103→        return initial_lr * (drop_rate ** (epoch // epochs_drop))
   104→
   105→    @staticmethod
   106→    def cosine_annealing(initial_lr: float, epoch: int, total_epochs: int):
   107→        return initial_lr * 0.5 * (1 + np.cos(np.pi * epoch / total_epochs))
   108→
   109→    @staticmethod
   110→    def warmup_cosine(initial_lr: float, epoch: int, warmup_epochs: int, total_epochs: int):
   111→        if epoch < warmup_epochs:
   112→            return initial_lr * epoch / warmup_epochs
   113→        return LearningRateScheduler.cosine_annealing(
   114→            initial_lr, epoch - warmup_epochs, total_epochs - warmup_epochs
   115→        )
   116→
   117→
   118→OPTIMIZERS = {"sgd": SGD, "adam": Adam, "rmsprop": RMSProp}
   119→

     1→"""TinyTorch training module.
     2→
     3→Extracted from CS249R Module 08: Training Loop.
     4→NumPy-only implementation for educational purposes.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import numpy as np
    10→
    11→from cohezion.tinytorch.tensor import Tensor
    12→from cohezion.tinytorch.losses import mse_loss, mse_loss_gradient
    13→
    14→
    15→class Trainer:
    16→    """Simple training loop for neural networks."""
    17→
    18→    def __init__(self, model, optimizer, loss_fn=mse_loss, loss_grad_fn=mse_loss_gradient):
    19→        self.model = model
    20→        self.optimizer = optimizer
    21→        self.loss_fn = loss_fn
    22→        self.loss_grad_fn = loss_grad_fn
    23→        self.history: dict[str, list[float]] = {"train_loss": [], "val_loss": []}
    24→
    25→    def train_epoch(self, dataloader) -> float:
    26→        """Train for one epoch, return average loss."""
    27→        total_loss = 0.0
    28→        n_batches = 0
    29→
    30→        for batch in dataloader:
    31→            x_batch, y_batch = batch[0], batch[1]
    32→
    33→            # Forward pass
    34→            predictions = self.model(x_batch)
    35→            loss = self.loss_fn(predictions, y_batch)
    36→
    37→            # Backward pass (compute gradients)
    38→            grad = self.loss_grad_fn(predictions, y_batch)
    39→            # Simplified: propagate gradient through model
    40→            gradients = self._compute_gradients(x_batch, grad)
    41→
    42→            # Update parameters
    43→            self.optimizer.step(gradients)
    44→
    45→            total_loss += loss.item()
    46→            n_batches += 1
    47→
    48→        avg_loss = total_loss / max(n_batches, 1)
    49→        self.history["train_loss"].append(avg_loss)
    50→        return avg_loss
    51→
    52→    def _compute_gradients(self, x: Tensor, output_grad: Tensor) -> list[Tensor]:
    53→        """Compute parameter gradients via chain rule (simplified)."""
    54→        gradients = []
    55→        if hasattr(self.model, "parameters"):
    56→            for param in self.model.parameters():
    57→                # Simplified gradient: scale by output gradient magnitude
    58→                grad = Tensor(np.ones_like(param.data) * output_grad.data.mean())
    59→                gradients.append(grad)
    60→        return gradients
    61→
    62→    def evaluate(self, dataloader) -> float:
    63→        """Evaluate model on validation data."""
    64→        total_loss = 0.0
    65→        n_batches = 0
    66→
    67→        for batch in dataloader:
    68→            x_batch, y_batch = batch[0], batch[1]
    69→            predictions = self.model(x_batch)
    70→            loss = self.loss_fn(predictions, y_batch)
    71→            total_loss += loss.item()
    72→            n_batches += 1
    73→
    74→        avg_loss = total_loss / max(n_batches, 1)
    75→        self.history["val_loss"].append(avg_loss)
    76→        return avg_loss
    77→
    78→    def fit(self, train_loader, val_loader=None, epochs: int = 10, verbose: bool = True) -> dict:
    79→        """Full training loop."""
    80→        for epoch in range(epochs):
    81→            train_loss = self.train_epoch(train_loader)
    82→
    83→            if val_loader is not None:
    84→                val_loss = self.evaluate(val_loader)
    85→                if verbose:
    86→                    print(f"Epoch {epoch + 1}/{epochs} - "
    87→                          f"train_loss: {train_loss:.4f}, val_loss: {val_loss:.4f}")
    88→            elif verbose:
    89→                print(f"Epoch {epoch + 1}/{epochs} - train_loss: {train_loss:.4f}")
    90→
    91→        return self.history
    92→
    93→
    94→class EarlyStopping:
    95→    """Stop training when validation loss stops improving."""
    96→
    97→    def __init__(self, patience: int = 5, min_delta: float = 0.0):
    98→        self.patience = patience
    99→        self.min_delta = min_delta
   100→        self.best_loss = float("inf")
   101→        self.counter = 0
   102→
   103→    def should_stop(self, val_loss: float) -> bool:
   104→        if val_loss < self.best_loss - self.min_delta:
   105→            self.best_loss = val_loss
   106→            self.counter = 0
   107→            return False
   108→        self.counter += 1
   109→        return self.counter >= self.patience
   110→
   111→
   112→def compute_accuracy(predictions: Tensor, targets: Tensor) -> float:
   113→    """Compute classification accuracy."""
   114→    if predictions.data.ndim > 1:
   115→        pred_classes = np.argmax(predictions.data, axis=-1)
   116→    else:
   117→        pred_classes = (predictions.data > 0.5).astype(int)
   118→    target_classes = targets.data.astype(int)
   119→    return float(np.mean(pred_classes == target_classes))
   120→

     1→"""TinyTorch convolutions module.
     2→
     3→Extracted from CS249R Module 09: Convolutional Neural Networks.
     4→NumPy-only implementation for educational purposes.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import numpy as np
    10→
    11→from cohezion.tinytorch.tensor import Tensor
    12→
    13→
    14→def conv2d(
    15→    input_tensor: Tensor,
    16→    kernel: Tensor,
    17→    stride: int = 1,
    18→    padding: int = 0,
    19→) -> Tensor:
    20→    """2D convolution operation.
    21→
    22→    input_tensor: shape (H, W) or (C_in, H, W)
    23→    kernel: shape (K_h, K_w) or (C_out, C_in, K_h, K_w)
    24→    """
    25→    data = input_tensor.data
    26→    k = kernel.data
    27→
    28→    # Handle simple 2D case
    29→    if data.ndim == 2 and k.ndim == 2:
    30→        return _conv2d_single(data, k, stride, padding)
    31→
    32→    # Handle multi-channel case
    33→    if data.ndim == 3 and k.ndim == 4:
    34→        return _conv2d_multi(data, k, stride, padding)
    35→
    36→    raise ValueError(f"Unsupported shapes: input {data.shape}, kernel {k.shape}")
    37→
    38→
    39→def _conv2d_single(data: np.ndarray, kernel: np.ndarray, stride: int, padding: int) -> Tensor:
    40→    """Single-channel 2D convolution."""
    41→    if padding > 0:
    42→        data = np.pad(data, padding, mode="constant")
    43→
    44→    h, w = data.shape
    45→    kh, kw = kernel.shape
    46→    out_h = (h - kh) // stride + 1
    47→    out_w = (w - kw) // stride + 1
    48→
    49→    output = np.zeros((out_h, out_w), dtype=np.float32)
    50→    for i in range(out_h):
    51→        for j in range(out_w):
    52→            region = data[i * stride:i * stride + kh, j * stride:j * stride + kw]
    53→            output[i, j] = np.sum(region * kernel)
    54→
    55→    return Tensor(output)
    56→
    57→
    58→def _conv2d_multi(data: np.ndarray, kernels: np.ndarray, stride: int, padding: int) -> Tensor:
    59→    """Multi-channel 2D convolution."""
    60→    c_out, c_in, kh, kw = kernels.shape
    61→
    62→    if padding > 0:
    63→        data = np.pad(data, ((0, 0), (padding, padding), (padding, padding)), mode="constant")
    64→
    65→    _, h, w = data.shape
    66→    out_h = (h - kh) // stride + 1
    67→    out_w = (w - kw) // stride + 1
    68→
    69→    output = np.zeros((c_out, out_h, out_w), dtype=np.float32)
    70→    for oc in range(c_out):
    71→        for i in range(out_h):
    72→            for j in range(out_w):
    73→                region = data[:, i * stride:i * stride + kh, j * stride:j * stride + kw]
    74→                output[oc, i, j] = np.sum(region * kernels[oc])
    75→
    76→    return Tensor(output)
    77→
    78→
    79→def max_pool2d(input_tensor: Tensor, kernel_size: int = 2, stride: int | None = None) -> Tensor:
    80→    """2D max pooling."""
    81→    if stride is None:
    82→        stride = kernel_size
    83→
    84→    data = input_tensor.data
    85→    if data.ndim == 2:
    86→        h, w = data.shape
    87→        out_h = (h - kernel_size) // stride + 1
    88→        out_w = (w - kernel_size) // stride + 1
    89→        output = np.zeros((out_h, out_w), dtype=np.float32)
    90→        for i in range(out_h):
    91→            for j in range(out_w):
    92→                region = data[i * stride:i * stride + kernel_size,
    93→                              j * stride:j * stride + kernel_size]
    94→                output[i, j] = np.max(region)
    95→        return Tensor(output)
    96→
    97→    if data.ndim == 3:
    98→        c, h, w = data.shape
    99→        out_h = (h - kernel_size) // stride + 1
   100→        out_w = (w - kernel_size) // stride + 1
   101→        output = np.zeros((c, out_h, out_w), dtype=np.float32)
   102→        for ch in range(c):
   103→            for i in range(out_h):
   104→                for j in range(out_w):
   105→                    region = data[ch, i * stride:i * stride + kernel_size,
   106→                                  j * stride:j * stride + kernel_size]
   107→                    output[ch, i, j] = np.max(region)
   108→        return Tensor(output)
   109→
   110→    raise ValueError(f"Expected 2D or 3D input, got {data.ndim}D")
   111→
   112→
   113→def avg_pool2d(input_tensor: Tensor, kernel_size: int = 2, stride: int | None = None) -> Tensor:
   114→    """2D average pooling."""
   115→    if stride is None:
   116→        stride = kernel_size
   117→
   118→    data = input_tensor.data
   119→    h, w = data.shape[-2], data.shape[-1]
   120→    out_h = (h - kernel_size) // stride + 1
   121→    out_w = (w - kernel_size) // stride + 1
   122→
   123→    if data.ndim == 2:
   124→        output = np.zeros((out_h, out_w), dtype=np.float32)
   125→        for i in range(out_h):
   126→            for j in range(out_w):
   127→                region = data[i * stride:i * stride + kernel_size,
   128→                              j * stride:j * stride + kernel_size]
   129→                output[i, j] = np.mean(region)
   130→        return Tensor(output)
   131→
   132→    raise ValueError(f"Expected 2D input, got {data.ndim}D")
   133→
   134→
   135→class Conv2d:
   136→    """Convolutional layer with learnable kernels."""
   137→
   138→    def __init__(self, in_channels: int, out_channels: int, kernel_size: int,
   139→                 stride: int = 1, padding: int = 0):
   140→        self.in_channels = in_channels
   141→        self.out_channels = out_channels
   142→        self.kernel_size = kernel_size
   143→        self.stride = stride
   144→        self.padding = padding
   145→
   146→        scale = np.sqrt(2.0 / (in_channels * kernel_size * kernel_size))
   147→        self.weights = Tensor(
   148→            np.random.randn(out_channels, in_channels, kernel_size, kernel_size) * scale
   149→        )
   150→        self.bias = Tensor(np.zeros(out_channels))
   151→
   152→    def forward(self, x: Tensor) -> Tensor:
   153→        result = conv2d(x, self.weights, self.stride, self.padding)
   154→        # Add bias per channel
   155→        for c in range(self.out_channels):
   156→            result.data[c] += self.bias.data[c]
   157→        return result
   158→
   159→    def __call__(self, x: Tensor) -> Tensor:
   160→        return self.forward(x)
   161→
   162→    def parameters(self) -> list[Tensor]:
   163→        return [self.weights, self.bias]
   164→
   165→
   166→class MaxPool2d:
   167→    """Max pooling layer."""
   168→
   169→    def __init__(self, kernel_size: int = 2, stride: int | None = None):
   170→        self.kernel_size = kernel_size
   171→        self.stride = stride
   172→
   173→    def forward(self, x: Tensor) -> Tensor:
   174→        return max_pool2d(x, self.kernel_size, self.stride)
   175→
   176→    def __call__(self, x: Tensor) -> Tensor:
   177→        return self.forward(x)
   178→

  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/.worktrees/spec-cs249r-book-integration-2ddb186d/src/cohezion/tinytorch/tokenization.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"TinyTorch tokenization module.\n\nExtracted from CS249R Module 10: Tokenization.\nNumPy-only implementation for educational purposes.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nfrom collections import Counter\n\n\nclass CharTokenizer:\n    \"\"\"Character-level tokenizer: each character is a token.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.char_to_idx: dict[str, int] = {}\n        self.idx_to_char: dict[int, str] = {}\n\n    def fit(self, text: str):\n        chars = sorted(set(text))\n        self.char_to_idx = {c: i for i, c in enumerate(chars)}\n        self.idx_to_char = {i: c for c, i in self.char_to_idx.items()}\n        return self\n\n    def encode(self, text: str) -> list[int]:\n        return [self.char_to_idx.get(c, 0) for c in text]\n\n    def decode(self, tokens: list[int]) -> str:\n        return \"\".join(self.idx_to_char.get(t, \"?\") for t in tokens)\n\n    @property\n    def vocab_size(self) -> int:\n        return len(self.char_to_idx)\n\n\nclass WordTokenizer:\n    \"\"\"Word-level tokenizer with vocabulary limit.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, vocab_size: int = 10000):\n        self.max_vocab = vocab_size\n        self.word_to_idx: dict[str, int] = {\"<PAD>\": 0, \"<UNK>\": 1}\n        self.idx_to_word: dict[int, str] = {0: \"<PAD>\", 1: \"<UNK>\"}\n\n    def fit(self, text: str):\n        words = re.findall(r\"\\w+|[^\\w\\s]\", text.lower())\n        counts = Counter(words).most_common(self.max_vocab - 2)\n        for i, (word, _) in enumerate(counts, start=2):\n            self.word_to_idx[word] = i\n            self.idx_to_word[i] = word\n        return self\n\n    def encode(self, text: str) -> list[int]:\n        words = re.findall(r\"\\w+|[^\\w\\s]\", text.lower())\n        return [self.word_to_idx.get(w, 1) for w in words]\n\n    def decode(self, tokens: list[int]) -> str:\n        return \" \".join(self.idx_to_word.get(t, \"<UNK>\") for t in tokens)\n\n    @property\n    def vocab_size(self) -> int:\n        return len(self.word_to_idx)\n\n\nclass BPETokenizer:\n    \"\"\"Byte-Pair Encoding tokenizer (simplified).\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, vocab_size: int = 1000):\n        self.target_vocab_size = vocab_size\n        self.merges: list[tuple[str, str]] = []\n        self.vocab: dict[str, int] = {}\n\n    def fit(self, text: str):\n        # Start with character-level tokens\n        words = [\" \".join(word) + \" </w>\" for word in text.split()]\n        vocab = Counter(words)\n\n        while len(self.vocab) < self.target_vocab_size:\n            pairs = self._get_pairs(vocab)\n            if not pairs:\n                break\n            best_pair = max(pairs, key=pairs.get)\n            self.merges.append(best_pair)\n            vocab = self._merge_pair(vocab, best_pair)\n\n        # Build final vocabulary\n        all_tokens = set()\n        for word in vocab:\n            all_tokens.update(word.split())\n        self.vocab = {token: i for i, token in enumerate(sorted(all_tokens))}\n        return self\n\n    def _get_pairs(self, vocab: dict) -> Counter:\n        pairs = Counter()\n        for word, count in vocab.items():\n            symbols = word.split()\n            for i in range(len(symbols) - 1):\n                pairs[(symbols[i], symbols[i + 1])] += count\n        return pairs\n\n    def _merge_pair(self, vocab: dict, pair: tuple[str, str]) -> dict:\n        new_vocab = {}\n        bigram = \" \".join(pair)\n        replacement = \"\".join(pair)\n        for word, count in vocab.items():\n            new_word = word.replace(bigram, replacement)\n            new_vocab[new_word] = count\n        return new_vocab\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/.worktrees/spec-cs249r-book-integration-2ddb186d/src/cohezion/tinytorch/embeddings.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"TinyTorch embeddings module.\n\nExtracted from CS249R Module 11: Embeddings.\nNumPy-only implementation for educational purposes.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport numpy as np\n\nfrom cohezion.tinytorch.tensor import Tensor\n\n\nclass Embedding:\n    \"\"\"Lookup table mapping token indices to dense vectors.\n\n    Each row of the embedding matrix is a learned vector representation\n    for that token index.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, num_embeddings: int, embedding_dim: int):\n        self.num_embeddings = num_embeddings\n        self.embedding_dim = embedding_dim\n        self.weight = Tensor(\n            np.random.randn(num_embeddings, embedding_dim).astype(np.float32) * 0.01\n        )\n\n    def forward(self, indices: Tensor) -> Tensor:\n        \"\"\"Look up embeddings for given indices.\"\"\"\n        return Tensor(self.weight.data[indices.data.astype(int)])\n\n    def __call__(self, indices: Tensor) -> Tensor:\n        return self.forward(indices)\n\n    def parameters(self) -> list[Tensor]:\n        return [self.weight]\n\n\nclass PositionalEncoding:\n    \"\"\"Sinusoidal positional encoding for Transformers.\n\n    PE(pos, 2i)   = sin(pos / 10000^(2i/d_model))\n    PE(pos, 2i+1) = cos(pos / 10000^(2i/d_model))\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, d_model: int, max_len: int = 5000):\n        self.d_model = d_model\n        pe = np.zeros((max_len, d_model), dtype=np.float32)\n        position = np.arange(0, max_len)[:, np.newaxis]\n        div_term = np.exp(np.arange(0, d_model, 2) * -(np.log(10000.0) / d_model))\n        pe[:, 0::2] = np.sin(position * div_term)\n        pe[:, 1::2] = np.cos(position * div_term[:d_model // 2])\n        self.pe = Tensor(pe)\n\n    def forward(self, x: Tensor) -> Tensor:\n        \"\"\"Add positional encoding to input embeddings.\"\"\"\n        seq_len = x.shape[-2] if x.data.ndim > 1 else x.shape[0]\n        return Tensor(x.data + self.pe.data[:seq_len])\n\n    def __call__(self, x: Tensor) -> Tensor:\n        return self.forward(x)\n\n\ndef cosine_similarity(a: Tensor, b: Tensor) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Compute cosine similarity between two vectors.\"\"\"\n    dot = np.dot(a.data.flatten(), b.data.flatten())\n    norm_a = np.linalg.norm(a.data)\n    norm_b = np.linalg.norm(b.data)\n    if norm_a == 0 or norm_b == 0:\n        return 0.0\n    return float(dot / (norm_a * norm_b))\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/.worktrees/spec-cs249r-book-integration-2ddb186d/src/cohezion/tinytorch/attention.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"TinyTorch attention module.\n\nExtracted from CS249R Module 12: Attention Mechanisms.\nNumPy-only implementation for educational purposes.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport numpy as np\n\nfrom cohezion.tinytorch.tensor import Tensor\n\n\ndef scaled_dot_product_attention(\n    query: Tensor, key: Tensor, value: Tensor, mask: Tensor | None = None\n) -> tuple[Tensor, Tensor]:\n    \"\"\"Scaled dot-product attention.\n\n    Attention(Q,K,V) = softmax(QK^T / sqrt(d_k)) V\n\n    Returns (output, attention_weights).\n    \"\"\"\n    d_k = query.shape[-1]\n    scores = Tensor(query.data @ key.data.swapaxes(-2, -1) / np.sqrt(d_k))\n\n    if mask is not None:\n        scores = Tensor(np.where(mask.data, scores.data, -1e9))\n\n    # Softmax along last axis\n    shifted = scores.data - scores.data.max(axis=-1, keepdims=True)\n    exp_scores = np.exp(shifted)\n    weights = exp_scores / exp_scores.sum(axis=-1, keepdims=True)\n\n    output = Tensor(weights @ value.data)\n    return output, Tensor(weights)\n\n\nclass MultiHeadAttention:\n    \"\"\"Multi-head attention mechanism.\n\n    Splits queries, keys, values into multiple heads,\n    applies attention independently, then concatenates.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, d_model: int, num_heads: int):\n        assert d_model % num_heads == 0\n        self.d_model = d_model\n        self.num_heads = num_heads\n        self.head_dim = d_model // num_heads\n\n        scale = np.sqrt(2.0 / (d_model + d_model))\n        self.w_q = Tensor(np.random.randn(d_model, d_model).astype(np.float32) * scale)\n        self.w_k = Tensor(np.random.randn(d_model, d_model).astype(np.float32) * scale)\n        self.w_v = Tensor(np.random.randn(d_model, d_model).astype(np.float32) * scale)\n        self.w_o = Tensor(np.random.randn(d_model, d_model).astype(np.float32) * scale)\n\n    def forward(self, query: Tensor, key: Tensor, value: Tensor,\n                mask: Tensor | None = None) -> Tensor:\n        \"\"\"Multi-head attention forward pass.\"\"\"\n        # Project Q, K, V\n        q = Tensor(query.data @ self.w_q.data)\n        k = Tensor(key.data @ self.w_k.data)\n        v = Tensor(value.data @ self.w_v.data)\n\n        # Split into heads\n        q = self._split_heads(q)\n        k = self._split_heads(k)\n        v = self._split_heads(v)\n\n        # Apply attention per head\n        attn_output, _ = scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, mask)\n\n        # Concatenate heads\n        output = self._combine_heads(attn_output)\n\n        # Final linear projection\n        return Tensor(output.data @ self.w_o.data)\n\n    def _split_heads(self, x: Tensor) -> Tensor:\n        \"\"\"Reshape to (batch, heads, seq_len, head_dim) or (heads, seq_len, head_dim).\"\"\"\n        if x.data.ndim == 2:\n            seq_len = x.shape[0]\n            reshaped = x.data.reshape(seq_len, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)\n            return Tensor(reshaped.transpose(1, 0, 2))\n        batch, seq_len = x.shape[0], x.shape[1]\n        reshaped = x.data.reshape(batch, seq_len, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)\n        return Tensor(reshaped.transpose(0, 2, 1, 3))\n\n    def _combine_heads(self, x: Tensor) -> Tensor:\n        \"\"\"Reverse of split_heads.\"\"\"\n        if x.data.ndim == 3:\n            transposed = x.data.transpose(1, 0, 2)\n            return Tensor(transposed.reshape(transposed.shape[0], -1))\n        transposed = x.data.transpose(0, 2, 1, 3)\n        batch, seq_len = transposed.shape[0], transposed.shape[1]\n        return Tensor(transposed.reshape(batch, seq_len, -1))\n\n    def __call__(self, query: Tensor, key: Tensor, value: Tensor,\n                 mask: Tensor | None = None) -> Tensor:\n        return self.forward(query, key, value, mask)\n\n    def parameters(self) -> list[Tensor]:\n        return [self.w_q, self.w_k, self.w_v, self.w_o]\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/.worktrees/spec-cs249r-book-integration-2ddb186d/src/cohezion/tinytorch/transformers.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"TinyTorch transformers module.\n\nExtracted from CS249R Module 13: Transformer Architecture.\nNumPy-only implementation for educational purposes.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport numpy as np\n\nfrom cohezion.tinytorch.tensor import Tensor\nfrom cohezion.tinytorch.attention import MultiHeadAttention\nfrom cohezion.tinytorch.layers import Linear\n\n\nclass LayerNorm:\n    \"\"\"Layer normalization: normalize across features (not batch).\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, d_model: int, eps: float = 1e-6):\n        self.d_model = d_model\n        self.eps = eps\n        self.gamma = Tensor(np.ones(d_model, dtype=np.float32))\n        self.beta = Tensor(np.zeros(d_model, dtype=np.float32))\n\n    def forward(self, x: Tensor) -> Tensor:\n        mean = x.data.mean(axis=-1, keepdims=True)\n        var = x.data.var(axis=-1, keepdims=True)\n        x_norm = (x.data - mean) / np.sqrt(var + self.eps)\n        return Tensor(self.gamma.data * x_norm + self.beta.data)\n\n    def __call__(self, x: Tensor) -> Tensor:\n        return self.forward(x)\n\n\nclass FeedForward:\n    \"\"\"Position-wise feed-forward network: FFN(x) = ReLU(xW1+b1)W2+b2.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, d_model: int, d_ff: int | None = None):\n        d_ff = d_ff or d_model * 4\n        self.linear1 = Linear(d_model, d_ff)\n        self.linear2 = Linear(d_ff, d_model)\n\n    def forward(self, x: Tensor) -> Tensor:\n        hidden = self.linear1(x)\n        activated = Tensor(np.maximum(0, hidden.data))  # ReLU\n        return self.linear2(activated)\n\n    def __call__(self, x: Tensor) -> Tensor:\n        return self.forward(x)\n\n    def parameters(self) -> list[Tensor]:\n        return self.linear1.parameters() + self.linear2.parameters()\n\n\nclass TransformerEncoderLayer:\n    \"\"\"Single transformer encoder layer: self-attention + feed-forward.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, d_model: int, num_heads: int, d_ff: int | None = None):\n        self.attention = MultiHeadAttention(d_model, num_heads)\n        self.feed_forward = FeedForward(d_model, d_ff)\n        self.norm1 = LayerNorm(d_model)\n        self.norm2 = LayerNorm(d_model)\n\n    def forward(self, x: Tensor, mask: Tensor | None = None) -> Tensor:\n        # Self-attention with residual connection and layer norm\n        attn_out = self.attention(x, x, x, mask)\n        x = self.norm1(Tensor(x.data + attn_out.data))\n\n        # Feed-forward with residual connection and layer norm\n        ff_out = self.feed_forward(x)\n        x = self.norm2(Tensor(x.data + ff_out.data))\n        return x\n\n    def __call__(self, x: Tensor, mask: Tensor | None = None) -> Tensor:\n        return self.forward(x, mask)\n\n    def parameters(self) -> list[Tensor]:\n        params = self.attention.parameters()\n        params += self.feed_forward.parameters()\n        params += [self.norm1.gamma, self.norm1.beta]\n        params += [self.norm2.gamma, self.norm2.beta]\n        return params\n\n\nclass TransformerEncoder:\n    \"\"\"Stack of transformer encoder layers.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, d_model: int, num_heads: int, num_layers: int, d_ff: int | None = None):\n        self.layers = [\n            TransformerEncoderLayer(d_model, num_heads, d_ff)\n            for _ in range(num_layers)\n        ]\n\n    def forward(self, x: Tensor, mask: Tensor | None = None) -> Tensor:\n        for layer in self.layers:\n            x = layer(x, mask)\n        return x\n\n    def __call__(self, x: Tensor, mask: Tensor | None = None) -> Tensor:\n        return self.forward(x, mask)\n\n    def parameters(self) -> list[Tensor]:\n        return [p for layer in self.layers for p in layer.parameters()]\n"
}

  "content": "\"\"\"TinyTorch profiling module.\n\nExtracted from CS249R Module 14: Profiling.\nNumPy-only implementation for educational purposes.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport time\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n\nimport numpy as np\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ProfileResult:\n    \"\"\"Result of profiling a single operation.\"\"\"\n    name: str\n    time_ms: float\n    memory_bytes: int = 0\n    flops: int = 0\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ModelProfile:\n    \"\"\"Aggregate profile of a model.\"\"\"\n    results: list[ProfileResult] = field(default_factory=list)\n\n    @property\n    def total_time_ms(self) -> float:\n        return sum(r.time_ms for r in self.results)\n\n    @property\n    def total_memory_bytes(self) -> int:\n        return sum(r.memory_bytes for r in self.results)\n\n    def summary(self) -> str:\n        lines = [f\"{'Layer':<30} {'Time (ms)':<12} {'Memory (KB)':<12}\"]\n        lines.append(\"-\" * 54)\n        for r in self.results:\n            lines.append(f\"{r.name:<30} {r.time_ms:<12.3f} {r.memory_bytes / 1024:<12.1f}\")\n        lines.append(\"-\" * 54)\n        lines.append(f\"{'TOTAL':<30} {self.total_time_ms:<12.3f} {self.total_memory_bytes / 1024:<12.1f}\")\n        return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n\n\ndef time_operation(func, *args, warmup: int = 5, iterations: int = 50, **kwargs) -> ProfileResult:\n    \"\"\"Time a function with warmup and statistical analysis.\"\"\"\n    for _ in range(warmup):\n        func(*args, **kwargs)\n\n    times = []\n    for _ in range(iterations):\n        start = time.perf_counter_ns()\n        result = func(*args, **kwargs)\n        end = time.perf_counter_ns()\n        times.append((end - start) / 1e6)\n\n    return ProfileResult(\n        name=getattr(func, \"__name__\", str(func)),\n        time_ms=float(np.median(times)),\n    )\n\n\ndef estimate_flops_linear(in_features: int, out_features: int, batch_size: int = 1) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Estimate FLOPs for a linear layer: 2 * batch * in * out.\"\"\"\n    return 2 * batch_size * in_features * out_features\n\n\ndef estimate_flops_conv2d(\n    in_channels: int, out_channels: int, kernel_size: int,\n    output_h: int, output_w: int\n) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Estimate FLOPs for a conv2d layer.\"\"\"\n    return 2 * out_channels * in_channels * kernel_size * kernel_size * output_h * output_w\n\n\ndef estimate_memory(shape: tuple, dtype_bytes: int = 4) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Estimate memory usage for a tensor.\"\"\"\n    return int(np.prod(shape)) * dtype_bytes\n\n\ndef profile_model(model, sample_input, warmup: int = 5, iterations: int = 50) -> ModelProfile:\n    \"\"\"Profile a model's forward pass layer by layer.\"\"\"\n    profile = ModelProfile()\n\n    if hasattr(model, \"layers\"):\n        x = sample_input\n        for i, layer in enumerate(model.layers):\n            result = time_operation(layer, x, warmup=warmup, iterations=iterations)\n            result.name = f\"Layer {i}: {type(layer).__name__}\"\n            if hasattr(x, \"data\"):\n                result.memory_bytes = estimate_memory(x.data.shape)\n            profile.results.append(result)\n            x = layer(x)\n    else:\n        result = time_operation(model, sample_input, warmup=warmup, iterations=iterations)\n        result.name = type(model).__name__\n        profile.results.append(result)\n\n    return profile\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/.worktrees/spec-cs249r-book-integration-2ddb186d/src/cohezion/tinytorch/quantization.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"TinyTorch quantization module.\n\nExtracted from CS249R Module 15: Quantization.\nNumPy-only implementation for educational purposes.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n\nimport numpy as np\n\nfrom cohezion.tinytorch.tensor import Tensor\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass QuantizedTensor:\n    \"\"\"Tensor quantized to integer representation.\"\"\"\n    data: np.ndarray  # Integer values\n    scale: float\n    zero_point: float\n    num_bits: int\n    original_shape: tuple\n\n\ndef quantize(tensor: Tensor, num_bits: int = 8) -> QuantizedTensor:\n    \"\"\"Quantize a floating-point tensor to fixed-point integer.\"\"\"\n    qmin = -(2 ** (num_bits - 1))\n    qmax = 2 ** (num_bits - 1) - 1\n\n    min_val = float(tensor.data.min())\n    max_val = float(tensor.data.max())\n\n    # Compute scale and zero point\n    scale = (max_val - min_val) / (qmax - qmin) if max_val != min_val else 1.0\n    zero_point = qmin - min_val / scale\n\n    # Quantize\n    q_data = np.round(tensor.data / scale + zero_point).clip(qmin, qmax)\n\n    return QuantizedTensor(\n        data=q_data.astype(np.int8 if num_bits <= 8 else np.int16),\n        scale=scale,\n        zero_point=zero_point,\n        num_bits=num_bits,\n        original_shape=tensor.shape,\n    )\n\n\ndef dequantize(qt: QuantizedTensor) -> Tensor:\n    \"\"\"Convert quantized tensor back to floating-point (approximate).\"\"\"\n    data = (qt.data.astype(np.float32) - qt.zero_point) * qt.scale\n    return Tensor(data)\n\n\ndef quantize_model_weights(model, num_bits: int = 8) -> dict[str, QuantizedTensor]:\n    \"\"\"Quantize all model parameters.\"\"\"\n    quantized = {}\n    if hasattr(model, \"parameters\"):\n        for i, param in enumerate(model.parameters()):\n            quantized[f\"param_{i}\"] = quantize(param, num_bits)\n    return quantized\n\n\ndef compute_quantization_error(original: Tensor, num_bits: int = 8) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Measure the error introduced by quantization.\"\"\"\n    qt = quantize(original, num_bits)\n    restored = dequantize(qt)\n    mse = float(np.mean((original.data - restored.data) ** 2))\n    return mse\n\n\ndef compression_ratio(original_bits: int, quantized_bits: int) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Calculate the compression ratio.\"\"\"\n    return original_bits / quantized_bits\n\n\ndef summarize_quantization(tensor: Tensor, bits_list: list[int] | None = None) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Compare quantization quality across different bit widths.\"\"\"\n    if bits_list is None:\n        bits_list = [8, 4, 2]\n\n    results = {}\n    for bits in bits_list:\n        error = compute_quantization_error(tensor, bits)\n        ratio = compression_ratio(32, bits)\n        results[f\"int{bits}\"] = {\n            \"error_mse\": error,\n            \"compression_ratio\": ratio,\n            \"size_reduction\": f\"{ratio:.0f}x\",\n        }\n    return results\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/.worktrees/spec-cs249r-book-integration-2ddb186d/src/cohezion/tinytorch/compression.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"TinyTorch compression module.\n\nExtracted from CS249R Module 16: Model Compression.\nNumPy-only implementation for educational purposes.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport numpy as np\n\nfrom cohezion.tinytorch.tensor import Tensor\n\n\ndef magnitude_pruning(weights: Tensor, sparsity: float = 0.5) -> tuple[Tensor, Tensor]:\n    \"\"\"Prune weights by magnitude: zero out smallest values.\n\n    Returns (pruned_weights, binary_mask).\n    \"\"\"\n    threshold = np.percentile(np.abs(weights.data), sparsity * 100)\n    mask = (np.abs(weights.data) > threshold).astype(np.float32)\n    return Tensor(weights.data * mask), Tensor(mask)\n\n\ndef structured_pruning(weights: Tensor, sparsity: float = 0.5, axis: int = 0) -> Tensor:\n    \"\"\"Prune entire rows/columns (structured) based on L2 norm.\"\"\"\n    norms = np.linalg.norm(weights.data, axis=axis)\n    n_keep = max(1, int(len(norms) * (1 - sparsity)))\n    keep_indices = np.argsort(norms)[-n_keep:]\n\n    if axis == 0:\n        return Tensor(weights.data[keep_indices])\n    return Tensor(weights.data[:, keep_indices])\n\n\ndef low_rank_approximation(weights: Tensor, rank: int) -> tuple[Tensor, Tensor]:\n    \"\"\"Approximate weight matrix using SVD truncation.\n\n    W ≈ U_r @ V_r^T where r << min(m, n).\n    Returns (U_r, V_r) such that U_r @ V_r ≈ W.\n    \"\"\"\n    U, S, Vt = np.linalg.svd(weights.data, full_matrices=False)\n    U_r = U[:, :rank] * np.sqrt(S[:rank])\n    V_r = Vt[:rank, :] * np.sqrt(S[:rank])[:, np.newaxis]\n    return Tensor(U_r), Tensor(V_r)\n\n\ndef compute_sparsity(weights: Tensor) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Compute fraction of zero weights.\"\"\"\n    return float(np.mean(weights.data == 0))\n\n\ndef count_parameters(model) -> dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"Count total and non-zero parameters in a model.\"\"\"\n    if not hasattr(model, \"parameters\"):\n        return {\"total\": 0, \"nonzero\": 0}\n\n    total = 0\n    nonzero = 0\n    for param in model.parameters():\n        total += param.size\n        nonzero += int(np.count_nonzero(param.data))\n\n    return {\n        \"total\": total,\n        \"nonzero\": nonzero,\n        \"pruned\": total - nonzero,\n        \"sparsity\": 1 - nonzero / total if total > 0 else 0,\n    }\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/.worktrees/spec-cs249r-book-integration-2ddb186d/src/cohezion/tinytorch/acceleration.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"TinyTorch acceleration module.\n\nExtracted from CS249R Module 17: Hardware Acceleration.\nNumPy-only implementation for educational purposes.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport time\n\nimport numpy as np\n\nfrom cohezion.tinytorch.tensor import Tensor\n\n\ndef fuse_conv_bn(conv_weights: Tensor, conv_bias: Tensor,\n                 bn_gamma: Tensor, bn_beta: Tensor,\n                 bn_mean: np.ndarray, bn_var: np.ndarray,\n                 eps: float = 1e-5) -> tuple[Tensor, Tensor]:\n    \"\"\"Fuse convolution and batch normalization into a single operation.\n\n    This eliminates the BN layer at inference time (same math, fewer ops).\n    \"\"\"\n    scale = bn_gamma.data / np.sqrt(bn_var + eps)\n\n    if conv_weights.data.ndim == 4:\n        fused_weights = Tensor(conv_weights.data * scale[:, None, None, None])\n    else:\n        fused_weights = Tensor(conv_weights.data * scale)\n\n    fused_bias = Tensor(bn_beta.data + (conv_bias.data - bn_mean) * scale)\n    return fused_weights, fused_bias\n\n\ndef im2col(input_data: np.ndarray, kernel_size: int, stride: int = 1, padding: int = 0) -> np.ndarray:\n    \"\"\"Convert image patches to columns for matrix multiplication.\n\n    Enables using optimized GEMM for convolution.\n    \"\"\"\n    if padding > 0:\n        input_data = np.pad(input_data, padding, mode=\"constant\")\n\n    h, w = input_data.shape[-2], input_data.shape[-1]\n    out_h = (h - kernel_size) // stride + 1\n    out_w = (w - kernel_size) // stride + 1\n\n    cols = []\n    for i in range(out_h):\n        for j in range(out_w):\n            patch = input_data[..., i * stride:i * stride + kernel_size,\n                               j * stride:j * stride + kernel_size]\n            cols.append(patch.flatten())\n\n    return np.array(cols, dtype=np.float32)\n\n\ndef conv2d_im2col(input_tensor: Tensor, kernel: Tensor,\n                  stride: int = 1, padding: int = 0) -> Tensor:\n    \"\"\"Convolution using im2col + GEMM (matrix multiplication).\n\n    Faster than nested loops on hardware with optimized BLAS.\n    \"\"\"\n    cols = im2col(input_tensor.data, kernel.shape[-1], stride, padding)\n    kernel_flat = kernel.data.reshape(kernel.shape[0], -1) if kernel.data.ndim > 2 else kernel.data.flatten()\n\n    if kernel.data.ndim <= 2:\n        result = cols @ kernel_flat\n        h = int(np.sqrt(len(result)))\n        return Tensor(result.reshape(h, -1))\n\n    result = cols @ kernel_flat.T\n    out_size = int(np.sqrt(cols.shape[0]))\n    return Tensor(result.T.reshape(kernel.shape[0], out_size, out_size))\n\n\ndef benchmark_operation(func, *args, iterations: int = 100, **kwargs) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Benchmark an operation with statistics.\"\"\"\n    times = []\n    for _ in range(iterations):\n        start = time.perf_counter_ns()\n        func(*args, **kwargs)\n        end = time.perf_counter_ns()\n        times.append((end - start) / 1e6)\n\n    times_arr = np.array(times)\n    return {\n        \"mean_ms\": float(np.mean(times_arr)),\n        \"median_ms\": float(np.median(times_arr)),\n        \"p95_ms\": float(np.percentile(times_arr, 95)),\n        \"p99_ms\": float(np.percentile(times_arr, 99)),\n        \"std_ms\": float(np.std(times_arr)),\n    }\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/.worktrees/spec-cs249r-book-integration-2ddb186d/src/cohezion/tinytorch/memoization.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"TinyTorch memoization module.\n\nExtracted from CS249R Module 18: Memoization and Caching.\nNumPy-only implementation for educational purposes.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hashlib\nfrom collections import OrderedDict\nfrom functools import wraps\n\nimport numpy as np\n\nfrom cohezion.tinytorch.tensor import Tensor\n\n\nclass TensorCache:\n    \"\"\"LRU cache for tensor computations.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, max_size: int = 128):\n        self.max_size = max_size\n        self.cache: OrderedDict[str, Tensor] = OrderedDict()\n        self.hits = 0\n        self.misses = 0\n\n    def _hash_key(self, *args) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Create a hash key from function arguments.\"\"\"\n        parts = []\n        for arg in args:\n            if isinstance(arg, Tensor):\n                parts.append(hashlib.md5(arg.data.tobytes()).hexdigest())\n            elif isinstance(arg, np.ndarray):\n                parts.append(hashlib.md5(arg.tobytes()).hexdigest())\n            else:\n                parts.append(str(arg))\n        return \"|\".join(parts)\n\n    def get(self, key: str) -> Tensor | None:\n        if key in self.cache:\n            self.hits += 1\n            self.cache.move_to_end(key)\n            return self.cache[key]\n        self.misses += 1\n        return None\n\n    def put(self, key: str, value: Tensor):\n        if key in self.cache:\n            self.cache.move_to_end(key)\n        self.cache[key] = value\n        if len(self.cache) > self.max_size:\n            self.cache.popitem(last=False)\n\n    @property\n    def hit_rate(self) -> float:\n        total = self.hits + self.misses\n        return self.hits / total if total > 0 else 0.0\n\n    def clear(self):\n        self.cache.clear()\n        self.hits = 0\n        self.misses = 0\n\n\ndef cached_operation(cache: TensorCache):\n    \"\"\"Decorator to cache tensor operation results.\"\"\"\n    def decorator(func):\n        @wraps(func)\n        def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):\n            key = cache._hash_key(*args)\n            result = cache.get(key)\n            if result is not None:\n                return result\n            result = func(*args, **kwargs)\n            if isinstance(result, Tensor):\n                cache.put(key, result)\n            return result\n        return wrapper\n    return decorator\n\n\nclass GradientCheckpoint:\n    \"\"\"Trade compute for memory: recompute activations during backward pass.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.checkpoints: dict[str, Tensor] = {}\n\n    def save(self, name: str, tensor: Tensor):\n        self.checkpoints[name] = Tensor(tensor.data.copy())\n\n    def load(self, name: str) -> Tensor | None:\n        return self.checkpoints.get(name)\n\n    def clear(self):\n        self.checkpoints.clear()\n\n    @property\n    def memory_bytes(self) -> int:\n        return sum(t.data.nbytes for t in self.checkpoints.values())\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/.worktrees/spec-cs249r-book-integration-2ddb186d/src/cohezion/tinytorch/benchmarking.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"TinyTorch benchmarking module.\n\nExtracted from CS249R Module 19: Benchmarking.\nNumPy-only implementation for educational purposes.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport time\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n\nimport numpy as np\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass BenchmarkResult:\n    \"\"\"Result of a benchmark run.\"\"\"\n    name: str\n    iterations: int\n    times_ms: list[float] = field(default_factory=list)\n\n    @property\n    def mean_ms(self) -> float:\n        return float(np.mean(self.times_ms))\n\n    @property\n    def median_ms(self) -> float:\n        return float(np.median(self.times_ms))\n\n    @property\n    def p95_ms(self) -> float:\n        return float(np.percentile(self.times_ms, 95))\n\n    @property\n    def p99_ms(self) -> float:\n        return float(np.percentile(self.times_ms, 99))\n\n    @property\n    def std_ms(self) -> float:\n        return float(np.std(self.times_ms))\n\n    @property\n    def throughput_qps(self) -> float:\n        return 1000.0 / self.mean_ms if self.mean_ms > 0 else 0.0\n\n    def summary(self) -> str:\n        return (f\"{self.name}: mean={self.mean_ms:.3f}ms, \"\n                f\"p95={self.p95_ms:.3f}ms, p99={self.p99_ms:.3f}ms, \"\n                f\"throughput={self.throughput_qps:.1f} QPS\")\n\n\ndef benchmark(func, *args, warmup: int = 10, iterations: int = 100,\n              name: str | None = None, **kwargs) -> BenchmarkResult:\n    \"\"\"Run a benchmark with warmup and statistical analysis.\"\"\"\n    name = name or getattr(func, \"__name__\", \"unknown\")\n\n    # Warmup\n    for _ in range(warmup):\n        func(*args, **kwargs)\n\n    # Timed runs\n    times = []\n    for _ in range(iterations):\n        start = time.perf_counter_ns()\n        func(*args, **kwargs)\n        end = time.perf_counter_ns()\n        times.append((end - start) / 1e6)\n\n    return BenchmarkResult(name=name, iterations=iterations, times_ms=times)\n\n\ndef compare_implementations(implementations: dict[str, callable],\n                            *args, warmup: int = 10, iterations: int = 100,\n                            **kwargs) -> dict[str, BenchmarkResult]:\n    \"\"\"Compare multiple implementations of the same operation.\"\"\"\n    results = {}\n    for name, func in implementations.items():\n        results[name] = benchmark(func, *args, warmup=warmup,\n                                  iterations=iterations, name=name, **kwargs)\n    return results\n\n\ndef benchmark_report(results: dict[str, BenchmarkResult]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Generate a comparison report from benchmark results.\"\"\"\n    lines = [f\"{'Implementation':<25} {'Mean (ms)':<12} {'P95 (ms)':<12} {'P99 (ms)':<12} {'QPS':<10}\"]\n    lines.append(\"-\" * 71)\n\n    baseline_time = None\n    for name, result in results.items():\n        if baseline_time is None:\n            baseline_time = result.mean_ms\n\n        speedup = baseline_time / result.mean_ms if result.mean_ms > 0 else 0\n        suffix = f\" ({speedup:.1f}x)\" if speedup != 1.0 else \" (baseline)\"\n\n        lines.append(\n            f\"{name:<25} {result.mean_ms:<12.3f} {result.p95_ms:<12.3f} \"\n            f\"{result.p99_ms:<12.3f} {result.throughput_qps:<10.1f}{suffix}\"\n        )\n\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n"
}

  "content": "\"\"\"Tests for TinyTorch advanced modules (10-19).\n\nValidates that extracted code is importable and key operations work.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport pytest\n\nfrom cohezion.tinytorch.tensor import Tensor\n\n\nclass TestTokenizationModule:\n    def test_import(self):\n        from cohezion.tinytorch import tokenization\n        assert tokenization is not None\n\n    def test_char_tokenizer(self):\n        from cohezion.tinytorch.tokenization import CharTokenizer\n        tok = CharTokenizer().fit(\"hello world\")\n        encoded = tok.encode(\"hello\")\n        assert len(encoded) == 5\n        assert tok.decode(encoded) == \"hello\"\n\n\nclass TestEmbeddingsModule:\n    def test_import(self):\n        from cohezion.tinytorch import embeddings\n        assert embeddings is not None\n\n    def test_embedding_lookup(self):\n        from cohezion.tinytorch.embeddings import Embedding\n        emb = Embedding(num_embeddings=100, embedding_dim=32)\n        indices = Tensor(np.array([0, 5, 10]))\n        result = emb(indices)\n        assert result.shape == (3, 32)\n\n\nclass TestAttentionModule:\n    def test_import(self):\n        from cohezion.tinytorch.attention import MultiHeadAttention\n        assert MultiHeadAttention is not None\n\n    def test_attention_forward(self):\n        from cohezion.tinytorch.attention import MultiHeadAttention\n        mha = MultiHeadAttention(d_model=64, num_heads=4)\n        x = Tensor(np.random.randn(8, 64).astype(np.float32))\n        output = mha(x, x, x)\n        assert output.shape == (8, 64)\n\n    def test_scaled_dot_product(self):\n        from cohezion.tinytorch.attention import scaled_dot_product_attention\n        q = Tensor(np.random.randn(4, 16).astype(np.float32))\n        k = Tensor(np.random.randn(4, 16).astype(np.float32))\n        v = Tensor(np.random.randn(4, 16).astype(np.float32))\n        output, weights = scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v)\n        assert output.shape == (4, 16)\n        np.testing.assert_almost_equal(weights.data.sum(axis=-1), 1.0, decimal=5)\n\n\nclass TestTransformersModule:\n    def test_import(self):\n        from cohezion.tinytorch.transformers import TransformerEncoder\n        assert TransformerEncoder is not None\n\n    def test_encoder(self):\n        from cohezion.tinytorch.transformers import TransformerEncoder\n        encoder = TransformerEncoder(d_model=32, num_heads=4, num_layers=2)\n        x = Tensor(np.random.randn(8, 32).astype(np.float32))\n        output = encoder(x)\n        assert output.shape == (8, 32)\n\n\nclass TestProfilingModule:\n    def test_import(self):\n        from cohezion.tinytorch import profiling\n        assert profiling is not None\n\n    def test_time_operation(self):\n        from cohezion.tinytorch.profiling import time_operation\n        result = time_operation(lambda: np.dot(np.ones(100), np.ones(100)),\n                                warmup=2, iterations=10)\n        assert result.time_ms >= 0\n\n\nclass TestQuantizationModule:\n    def test_import(self):\n        from cohezion.tinytorch import quantization\n        assert quantization is not None\n\n    def test_quantize_roundtrip(self):\n        from cohezion.tinytorch.quantization import quantize, dequantize\n        original = Tensor(np.random.randn(10, 10).astype(np.float32))\n        qt = quantize(original, num_bits=8)\n        restored = dequantize(qt)\n        # INT8 roundtrip should be close\n        assert np.allclose(original.data, restored.data, atol=0.1)\n\n\nclass TestCompressionModule:\n    def test_import(self):\n        from cohezion.tinytorch import compression\n        assert compression is not None\n\n    def test_magnitude_pruning(self):\n        from cohezion.tinytorch.compression import magnitude_pruning\n        w = Tensor(np.random.randn(10, 10).astype(np.float32))\n        pruned, mask = magnitude_pruning(w, sparsity=0.5)\n        assert np.mean(pruned.data == 0) >= 0.4  # ~50% zeroed\n\n\nclass TestAccelerationModule:\n    def test_import(self):\n        from cohezion.tinytorch import acceleration\n        assert acceleration is not None\n\n\nclass TestMemoizationModule:\n    def test_import(self):\n        from cohezion.tinytorch import memoization\n        assert memoization is not None\n\n    def test_tensor_cache(self):\n        from cohezion.tinytorch.memoization import TensorCache\n        cache = TensorCache(max_size=10)\n        cache.put(\"key1\", Tensor([1, 2, 3]))\n        result = cache.get(\"key1\")\n        assert result is not None\n        assert cache.hit_rate == 1.0\n\n\nclass TestBenchmarkingModule:\n    def test_import(self):\n        from cohezion.tinytorch import benchmarking\n        assert benchmarking is not None\n\n    def test_benchmark(self):\n        from cohezion.tinytorch.benchmarking import benchmark\n        result = benchmark(lambda: sum(range(100)), warmup=2, iterations=10,\n                           name=\"sum_test\")\n        assert result.mean_ms >= 0\n        assert result.name == \"sum_test\"\n"
}
